# PR-09 public API whitelist

This audit records every current named export of the four `aos-agent` package entries and `@aos-agent/agent-core` `.`, plus three documented root contracts missing from the current barrel. A keep decision requires a docs/README/example/eval import or an SDK/RPC type or constant promise; internal requires a resolved in-repository import; delete means no such consumer import was found and only the implementation/export declaration is cited.

## Counts

| Entry | Keep | Internal | Delete |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `.` | 115 | 707 | 207 |
| `./rpc-entry` | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| `./client` | 0 | 7 | 4 |
| `./external-connector` | 59 | 0 | 0 |
| `@aos-agent/agent-core` | 8 | 731 | 860 |

## Adjudications

- Root connector exception: `createAgentRuntimeCompositionFactory` and `createExternalConnectorRegistry` stay on `.` because `docs/external-agent-connector.md` and `docs/sdk.md` import them from `aos-agent`. The complete connector contract remains classified independently on `./external-connector`.
- `ExternalAgentConnector` stays on root and agent-core because SDK documentation promises that named contract. The full existing `./external-connector` surface stays keep under the L4 authority and the package README's supported-subpath promise.
- `SessionManager`, `Tool`, and `BashExecutionMessage` are documented root contracts missing from today's root barrel. They are keep targets, are recorded as additional rows, and Stage 2A must add the re-exports without changing their implementations.
- `SchedulerComposition`, `SchedulerCompositionOptions`, and `SchedulerCompositionFactory` are internal: no SDK/docs/example/eval import uses them, while repository tests or runtime composition import them.
- `./rpc-entry` is a script entry and has no named exports.
- `./client` has no public evidence in the audited docs/README/examples/evals set. Its repository-consumed names are internal and the remainder are delete.
- Agent-core foundation, scheduler, harness, and ledger names without public evidence are internal when another repository module imports them, otherwise delete. Delete means dropping a future public re-export, not deleting the implementing module.
